19	    raw: q.raw,
20	    recognized: q.recognized,
21	    filters: q.filters.map((f) => `${f.field}${f.op}${f.value}`),
22	    sort: q.sort ? `${q.sort.key}:${q.sort.dir}` : null,
23	    limit: q.limit ?? null,
24	    unavailable: q.unavailable.map((u) => `${u.kind}:${u.token}`),
25	  };
26	}
27	
28	// Demo data shown when no ?video=<id> is supplied. Shape matches the verified play_segments
29	// contract; only honest fields are surfaced. A real session loads from POST /api/query.
30	const DEMO_RALLIES: RallySegment[] = [
31	  { id: 1, start_time: 12, end_time: 20, duration: 8, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 9, shot_count_confidence: "High" } },
32	  { id: 2, start_time: 41, end_time: 47, duration: 6, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 5 } },
33	  { id: 3, start_time: 63, end_time: 84, duration: 21, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 19, shot_count_confidence: "Medium" } },
34	  { id: 4, start_time: 95, end_time: 101, duration: 6, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: {} },
35	  { id: 5, start_time: 130, end_time: 142, duration: 12, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 11 } },
36	  { id: 6, start_time: 158, end_time: 187, duration: 29, server: null, receiver: null, metadata: { shot_count: 24, shot_count_confidence: "High" } },
37	];
38	
39	type LoadState =
40	  | { status: "demo" }
41	  | { status: "loading" }
42	  | { status: "loaded"; count: number }
43	  | { status: "error"; error: string };
44	
45	export default function App() {
46	  const { t } = useTranslation();
47	  // Job-tethered (D10): a finished job's video_id arrives via ?video=<id>; absent ⇒ demo data.
48	  const videoId = useMemo(() => new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("video"), []);
49	
50	  const [rallies, setRallies] = useState<RallySegment[]>(videoId ? [] : DEMO_RALLIES);
51	  const [order, setOrder] = useState<RallySegment[]>(videoId ? [] : DEMO_RALLIES);
52	  const [loadState, setLoadState] = useState<LoadState>(videoId ? { status: "loading" } : { status: "demo" });
53	  const [reelName, setReelName] = useState("highlights");
54	  const [build, setBuild] = useState<BuildState>({ status: "idle" });
55	
56	  // Display order is separate from selection: a query can re-sort the cards while the shared
57	  // selection set (sel) stays the single source of truth for what lands in the reel.
58	  const sel = useRallySelection(rallies, "all");
59	
60	  // Load real rallies for a job's video (POST /api/query). Default ALL-selected (D8).
61	  useEffect(() => {
62	    if (!videoId) return;
63	    const cid = newCorrelationId();
64	    log("info", "rallies.load_start", { cid, video_id: videoId });
65	    let alive = true;
66	    listRallies(videoId, cid)
67	      .then((rs) => {
68	        if (!alive) return;
69	        setRallies(rs);
70	        setOrder(rs);
71	        sel.apply(rs.map((r) => r.id));
72	        setLoadState({ status: "loaded", count: rs.length });
73	        log("info", "rallies.loaded", { cid, video_id: videoId, count: rs.length });
74	      })
75	      .catch((err) => {
76	        if (!alive) return;
77	        setLoadState({ status: "error", error: String(err) });
78	        log("error", "rallies.load_failed", { cid, video_id: videoId, error: String(err) });
79	      });
80	    return () => {
81	      alive = false;
82	    };
83	    // videoId is stable for the page; sel.apply is a stable callback.
84	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
85	  }, [videoId]);
86	
87	  // Read the engine's stitching.min_shot_count so the footer can WARN before a selected rally is
88	  // silently dropped at compile (P4 / D7). Offline (no engine) is fine: no config ⇒ no warning.
89	  const [cfg, setCfg] = useState<EngineConfig | null>(null);
90	  useEffect(() => {
91	    let alive = true;
92	    getConfig()
93	      .then((c) => alive && setCfg(c))
94	      .catch((err) => log("warn", "config.unavailable", { error: String(err) }));
95	    return () => {
96	      alive = false;
97	    };
98	  }, []);
99	  const warning = floorRisk(rallies, sel.selected, cfg ? minShotCount(cfg) : undefined);
100	
101	  // Stable chronological ordinal (by start_time) so re-sorting never renumbers a rally.
102	  const ordinalOf = useMemo(() => {
103	    const m = new Map<number, number>();
104	    [...rallies].sort((a, b) => a.start_time - b.start_time).forEach((r, i) => m.set(r.id, i + 1));
105	    return (id: number) => m.get(id) ?? 0;
106	  }, [rallies]);
107	
108	  // Build → POST /api/compile → preview/download the stitched reel. One cid traces the whole call.
109	  const onBuild = async () => {
110	    if (build.status === "building") return;
111	    const cid = newCorrelationId();
112	    const filename = reelFilename(reelName);
113	    const ids = [...sel.selected];
114	    if (!videoId) {
115	      log("info", "build.no_video", { cid, selected: ids.length });
116	      setBuild({
117	        status: "error",
118	        error: t("build.noVideo"),
119	      });
120	      return;
121	    }
122	    setBuild({ status: "building" });
123	    log("info", "build.start", {
124	      cid,
125	      video_id: videoId,
126	      filename,
127	      selected: ids.length,
128	      total_sec: Math.round(sel.totalSec),
129	      below_floor: warning?.atRisk.length ?? 0,
130	    });
131	    try {
132	      const res = await compileReel(videoId, ids, filename, cid);
133	      const url = highlightUrl(videoId, filename);
134	      setBuild({ status: "done", url, filename });
135	      log("info", "build.done", { cid, video_id: videoId, filename, filepath: res.filepath, url });
136	    } catch (err) {
137	      const status = err instanceof ApiError ? err.status : undefined;
138	      const detail = err instanceof ApiError && err.body ? parseDetail(err.body) : String(err);
139	      setBuild({ status: "error", error: detail });
140	      log("error", "build.failed", { cid, video_id: videoId, filename, status, error: String(err) });
141	    }
142	  };
143	
144	  // Every query application is ONE correlatable journey: a `query.parse` line records exactly how
145	  // the text was understood, then a single outcome line (apply / skip / empty) shares the same cid.
146	  // Unsupported concepts and zero-result queries are logged loudly — never silently dropped.
147	  const onApplyQuery = (q: ParsedQuery, source: "typed" | "example") => {
148	    const cid = newCorrelationId();
149	    log("info", "query.parse", { cid, source, ...parseTrace(q) });
150	
151	    // Demand signal + honest "we ignored this": surface every shot-type/player/score the user asked
152	    // for but we can't yet honour (PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md §3 roadmap fields).
153	    if (q.unavailable.length > 0) {
154	      log("info", "query.unavailable_request", { cid, source, raw: q.raw, unavailable: q.unavailable });
155	    }
156	
157	    if (!q.recognized) {
158	      log("info", "query.skip", { cid, source, raw: q.raw, reason: "no actionable clause" });
159	      return; // empty / unparseable → leave the user's curation untouched
160	    }
161	
162	    try {
163	      const { ordered, selectedIds } = runQuery(rallies, q);
164	      setOrder(ordered);
165	      sel.apply(selectedIds);
166	      log("info", "query.apply", {
167	        cid,
168	        source,
169	        filters: q.filters.length,
170	        sort: q.sort ? `${q.sort.key}:${q.sort.dir}` : null,
171	        limit: q.limit ?? null,
172	        total: rallies.length,
173	        selected: selectedIds.length,
174	        selected_ids: selectedIds,
175	        dropped_unavailable: q.unavailable.map((u) => `${u.kind}:${u.token}`),
176	      });
177	      if (selectedIds.length === 0) {
178	        // Loud: a recognized query that matches nothing makes the grid look empty — say why.
179	        log("warn", "query.empty_result", { cid, source, ...parseTrace(q) });
180	      }
181	    } catch (err) {
182	      // Defensive: the pure parser/selector shouldn't throw, but never fail silently if it does.
183	      log("error", "query.apply_failed", { cid, source, raw: q.raw, error: String(err) });
184	    }
185	  };
186	
187	  // Manual curation edits are traced too, so a journey (query → hand-tune) reads as one story.
188	  const onToggle = (id: number) => {
189	    log("debug", "rally.toggle", { id, action: sel.isSelected(id) ? "remove" : "add" });
190	    sel.toggle(id);
191	  };
192	  const onBulk = (op: "select_all" | "clear" | "invert") => {
193	    const resultCount = op === "select_all" ? rallies.length : op === "clear" ? 0 : rallies.length - sel.count;
194	    log("info", "selection.bulk", { cid: newCorrelationId(), op, result_count: resultCount });
195	    if (op === "select_all") sel.selectAll();
196	    else if (op === "clear") sel.clearAll();
197	    else sel.invert();
198	  };
199	
200	  return (
201	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-paper text-ink pb-24">
202	      <header className="bg-ink text-white">
203	        <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-5xl items-center justify-between gap-3 px-6 py-4">
204	          <span className="text-xl font-extrabold tracking-tight">
205	            Khel<span className="text-green">▸</span>Sutra <span className="font-semibold text-lime">Studio</span>
206	          </span>
207	          <LanguageSwitcher />
208	        </div>
209	      </header>
210	
211	      <section className="mx-auto max-w-5xl px-6 py-10">
212	        <span className="inline-flex rounded-full bg-green/15 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-green">
213	          {t("app.badge")}
214	        </span>
215	        <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight">{t("app.title")}</h1>
216	        <p className="mt-2 max-w-2xl text-ink/70">
217	          <Trans i18nKey="app.intro" components={{ b: <strong />, i: <em /> }} />
218	        </p>
219	
220	        <ModeBanner load={loadState} videoId={videoId} />
221	
222	        <div className="mt-6">
223	          <QueryBar onApply={onApplyQuery} />
224	        </div>
225	
226	        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-sm">
227	          <button type="button" onClick={() => onBulk("select_all")} className="rounded-full border border-black/15 px-3 py-1 hover:border-green">{t("bulk.selectAll")}</button>
228	          <button type="button" onClick={() => onBulk("clear")} className="rounded-full border border-black/15 px-3 py-1 hover:border-green">{t("bulk.clear")}</button>
229	          <button type="button" onClick={() => onBulk("invert")} className="rounded-full border border-black/15 px-3 py-1 hover:border-green">{t("bulk.invert")}</button>
230	        </div>
231	
232	        <div className="mt-5">
233	          <RallyGrid rallies={order} isSelected={sel.isSelected} onToggle={onToggle} ordinalOf={ordinalOf} />
234	        </div>
235	
236	        <CompileResult build={build} onDismiss={() => setBuild({ status: "idle" })} />
237	      </section>
238	
239	      <SelectionFooter
240	        count={sel.count}
241	        totalSec={sel.totalSec}
242	        reelName={reelName}
243	        onReelNameChange={setReelName}
244	        onBuild={onBuild}
245	        building={build.status === "building"}
246	        warning={warning}
247	      />
248	    </main>
249	  );
250	}

252	/** Pull FastAPI's {"detail": "..."} message out of an error body, else show it raw. */
253	function parseDetail(body: string): string {
254	  try {
255	    const j = JSON.parse(body);
256	    if (j && typeof j.detail === "string") return j.detail;
257	  } catch {
258	    /* not JSON — fall through */
259	  }
260	  return body;
261	}
262	
263	function ModeBanner({ load, videoId }: { load: LoadState; videoId: string | null }) {
264	  if (load.status === "demo") {
265	    return (
266	      <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-ink/50" data-testid="mode-banner">
267	        <Trans i18nKey="mode.demo" components={{ code: <code /> }} />
268	      </p>
269	    );
270	  }
271	  if (load.status === "loading") {
272	    return (
273	      <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-ink/60" data-testid="mode-banner">
274	        <Trans i18nKey="mode.loading" values={{ videoId }} components={{ code: <code /> }} />
275	      </p>
276	    );
277	  }
278	  if (load.status === "error") {
279	    return (
280	      <p className="mt-3 rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-800" data-testid="mode-banner">
281	        <Trans i18nKey="mode.error" values={{ videoId, error: load.error }} components={{ code: <code /> }} />
282	      </p>
283	    );
284	  }
285	  return (
286	    <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-ink/50" data-testid="mode-banner">
287	      <Trans i18nKey="mode.loaded" values={{ count: load.count }} components={{ b: <strong className="text-ink/70" /> }} />
288	    </p>
289	  );
290	}

### 190. user (2026-06-21T09:52:43.345Z)

1	// Typed client for the engine FastAPI. SAME-ORIGIN relative `/api/*` (the appliance serves
2	// UI + engine behind one origin; in dev Vite proxies `/api` → :8000). Every call carries a
3	// correlation id (header + logs) so a journey is traceable end to end.
4	import { log, newCorrelationId } from "../lib/log";
5	import type { RallySegment, QueryResponse, CompileResponse, EngineConfig } from "./types";
6	
7	const BASE = "/api";
8	
9	export class ApiError extends Error {
10	  readonly cid?: string;
11	  readonly status?: number;
12	  readonly body?: string;
13	  constructor(message: string, opts: { cid?: string; status?: number; body?: string; cause?: unknown } = {}) {
14	    super(message);
15	    this.name = "ApiError";
16	    this.cid = opts.cid;
17	    this.status = opts.status;
18	    this.body = opts.body;
19	    if (opts.cause !== undefined) (this as { cause?: unknown }).cause = opts.cause;
20	  }
21	}
22	
23	interface RequestOpts {
24	  method?: string;
25	  body?: string;
26	  headers?: Record<string, string>;
27	  /** Correlation id to thread through this call; auto-generated if absent. */
28	  cid?: string;
29	}
30	
31	async function request<T>(path: string, opts: RequestOpts = {}): Promise<T> {
32	  const cid = opts.cid ?? newCorrelationId();
33	  const url = `${BASE}${path}`;
34	  const method = opts.method ?? "GET";
35	  log("info", "api.request", { cid, method, url });
36	
37	  let res: Response;
38	  try {
39	    res = await fetch(url, {
40	      method,
41	      body: opts.body,
42	      headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "x-correlation-id": cid, ...(opts.headers ?? {}) },
43	    });
44	  } catch (err) {
45	    // Loud failure, never silent (LOCAL_APPLIANCE_ARCHITECTURE.md §5).
46	    log("error", "api.network_error", { cid, method, url, error: String(err) });
47	    throw new ApiError(`Network error calling ${method} ${url}`, { cid, cause: err });
48	  }
49	
50	  if (!res.ok) {
51	    const body = await res.text().catch(() => "");
52	    log("error", "api.http_error", { cid, method, url, status: res.status, body: body.slice(0, 500) });
53	    throw new ApiError(`${method} ${url} failed: ${res.status}`, { cid, status: res.status, body });
54	  }
55	
56	  const data = (await res.json()) as T;
57	  log("info", "api.response", { cid, method, url, status: res.status });
58	  return data;
59	}
60	
61	/** List a video's detected rallies. POST /api/query {video_id} → {play_segments} [main.py:331]. */
62	export async function listRallies(videoId: string, cid?: string): Promise<RallySegment[]> {
63	  const data = await request<QueryResponse>("/query", {
64	    method: "POST",
65	    body: JSON.stringify({ video_id: videoId }),
66	    cid,
67	  });
68	  return data.play_segments ?? [];
69	}
70	
71	/** Stitch selected rallies into one reel. POST /api/compile {video_id, segment_ids, output_filename} [main.py:342]. */
72	export async function compileReel(
73	  videoId: string,
74	  segmentIds: number[],
75	  outputFilename: string,
76	  cid?: string,
77	): Promise<CompileResponse> {
78	  return request<CompileResponse>("/compile", {
79	    method: "POST",
80	    body: JSON.stringify({ video_id: videoId, segment_ids: segmentIds, output_filename: outputFilename }),
81	    cid,
82	  });
83	}
84	
85	/** The stream/download URL for a compiled reel. GET /api/highlights/{video_id}/{filename} [main.py:307]. */
86	export function highlightUrl(videoId: string, filename: string): string {
87	  return `${BASE}/highlights/${encodeURIComponent(videoId)}/${encodeURIComponent(filename)}`;
88	}
89	
90	/** Read engine config (for the stitching floor). GET /api/config [main.py:121]. */
91	export async function getConfig(cid?: string): Promise<EngineConfig> {
92	  return request<EngineConfig>("/config", { method: "GET", cid });
93	}
94	
95	/** The shot-count floor /api/compile silently enforces — the UI must warn, not hide [main.py:362]. */
96	export function minShotCount(cfg: EngineConfig): number | undefined {
97	  return cfg.stitching?.min_shot_count;
98	}

### 193. user (2026-06-21T09:53:02.865Z)

1	// i18n scaffold (tracker B1) — the shared localization foundation for KhelSutra Studio.
2	//
3	// Per docs/FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md + the engine's I18N_PLAN.md:
4	//   - keys, never prose (t("app.title"), enforced later by a no-bare-strings lint, B2);
5	//   - ICU MessageFormat for interpolation/plurals (Kannada/Hindi plural rules live in the catalog,
6	//     not the code — replaces the SPA's hand-rolled `count > 1 ? "s" : ""`);
7	//   - one negotiation order shared with the marketing site (B3);
8	//   - always-fallback to `en` so a missing translation degrades to English, never a raw key.
9	import i18n from "i18next";
10	import { initReactI18next } from "react-i18next";
11	import LanguageDetector from "i18next-browser-languagedetector";
12	import ICU from "i18next-icu";
13	import enCommon from "./locales/en/common.json";
14	import knCommon from "./locales/kn/common.json";
15	import hiCommon from "./locales/hi/common.json";
16	
17	// Supported locales (D6): `en` + `kn` + `hi`. Anything else falls back to `en`.
18	export const SUPPORTED_LOCALES = ["en", "kn", "hi"] as const;
19	export type Locale = (typeof SUPPORTED_LOCALES)[number];
20	export const DEFAULT_LOCALE: Locale = "en";
21	
22	// ONE persisted-preference key, shared with the marketing site's t() shim (D9 / tracker B3), so a
23	// visitor's language choice carries across both surfaces.
24	export const LANG_STORAGE_KEY = "khelsutra.lang";
25	
26	// Catalogs are co-located here (D9). `kn` + `hi` shipped (B5/B6, AI-drafted → native review
27	// pending). A missing key in any locale still falls back to `en` — the by-design invariant.
28	const resources = {
29	  en: { common: enCommon },
30	  kn: { common: knCommon },
31	  hi: { common: hiCommon },
32	} as const;
33	
34	void i18n
35	  .use(ICU)
36	  .use(LanguageDetector)
37	  .use(initReactI18next)
38	  .init({
39	    resources,
40	    fallbackLng: DEFAULT_LOCALE,
41	    supportedLngs: [...SUPPORTED_LOCALES],
42	    nonExplicitSupportedLngs: true, // en-IN -> en
43	    defaultNS: "common",
44	    ns: ["common"],
45	    interpolation: { escapeValue: false }, // React already escapes
46	    returnNull: false,
47	    // Negotiation order (I18N_PLAN §2.3): persisted pref -> ?lang= -> browser -> en.
48	    detection: {
49	      order: ["localStorage", "querystring", "navigator"],
50	      lookupLocalStorage: LANG_STORAGE_KEY,
51	      lookupQuerystring: "lang",
52	      caches: ["localStorage"],
53	    },
54	    react: { useSuspense: false },
55	  });
56	
57	// Keep <html lang> in sync with the active locale (screen readers + SEO use it). Reflects the
58	// resolved content language, so an `en` fallback for an absent locale still reads as `en`.
59	function syncDocumentLang(lng: string) {
60	  if (typeof document !== "undefined") {
61	    document.documentElement.lang = (i18n.resolvedLanguage || lng || DEFAULT_LOCALE).split("-")[0];
62	  }
63	}
64	i18n.on("languageChanged", syncDocumentLang);
65	syncDocumentLang(i18n.language ?? DEFAULT_LOCALE);
66	
67	export default i18n;
68

1	// CI guardrails that keep i18n "by design" (FRIENDLY_AND_MULTILINGUAL.md §4):
2	//   1. KEY-PARITY — every locale catalog has EXACTLY the `en` key set (the extensibility proof:
3	//      drop a stub locale and this lists every gap). Trivially green while only `en` exists; it
4	//      starts enforcing the moment a kn/hi catalog lands (B5/B6).
5	//   2. NO-BARE-STRINGS (attribute level, v1) — user-facing component attributes (placeholder /
6	//      aria-label / title) must route through t(), never a hardcoded literal. A curated start per
7	//      the doc; an AST-level sweep of JSX text can extend it later.
8	import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
9	import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
10	import { join } from "node:path";
11	
12	const LOCALES_DIR = join(import.meta.dirname, "locales");
13	const SRC_DIR = join(import.meta.dirname, "..");
14	
15	function flatten(obj: Record<string, unknown>, prefix = ""): string[] {
16	  const keys: string[] = [];
17	  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(obj)) {
18	    const key = prefix ? `${prefix}.${k}` : k;
19	    if (v && typeof v === "object" && !Array.isArray(v)) keys.push(...flatten(v as Record<string, unknown>, key));
20	    else keys.push(key);
21	  }
22	  return keys.sort();
23	}
24	
25	const loadKeys = (locale: string): string[] =>
26	  flatten(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(LOCALES_DIR, locale, "common.json"), "utf8")) as Record<string, unknown>);
27	
28	describe("i18n key-parity — every locale has EXACTLY the en keys", () => {
29	  const enKeys = loadKeys("en");
30	  const others = readdirSync(LOCALES_DIR, { withFileTypes: true })
31	    .filter((d) => d.isDirectory() && d.name !== "en")
32	    .map((d) => d.name);
33	
34	  it("en is the non-empty source catalog", () => {
35	    expect(enKeys.length).toBeGreaterThan(20);
36	  });
37	
38	  for (const loc of others) {
39	    it(`${loc} matches en exactly (no missing, no extra keys)`, () => {
40	      const keys = loadKeys(loc);
41	      const missing = enKeys.filter((k) => !keys.includes(k));
42	      const extra = keys.filter((k) => !enKeys.includes(k));
43	      expect({ locale: loc, missing, extra }).toEqual({ locale: loc, missing: [], extra: [] });
44	    });
45	  }
46	});
47	
48	describe("no-bare-strings — user-facing component attributes go through t()", () => {
49	  const FILES = [
50	    "App.tsx",
51	    "components/QueryBar.tsx",
52	    "components/RallyGrid.tsx",
53	    "components/SelectionFooter.tsx",
54	    "components/CompileResult.tsx",
55	    "components/LanguageSwitcher.tsx",
56	  ];
57	  // A hardcoded placeholder / aria-label / title with letters is a bare-string regression — it
58	  // must be an expression ({t(...)}), not a quoted literal.
59	  const BARE_ATTR = /\b(?:placeholder|aria-label|title)\s*=\s*"[^"]*[A-Za-z]{2}[^"]*"/g;
60	
61	  for (const f of FILES) {
62	    it(`${f}: no hardcoded placeholder/aria-label/title`, () => {
63	      const src = readFileSync(join(SRC_DIR, f), "utf8");
64	      expect(src.match(BARE_ATTR) ?? []).toEqual([]);
65	    });
66	  }
67	});
68	
69	describe("Indic fonts (B4) — self-hosted OFL Noto, no runtime CDN", () => {
70	  it("main.tsx bundles the OFL Noto Kannada + Devanagari fonts (self-hosted, not Google Fonts)", () => {
71	    const main = readFileSync(join(SRC_DIR, "main.tsx"), "utf8");
72	    expect(main).toMatch(/@fontsource-variable\/noto-sans-kannada/);
73	    expect(main).toMatch(/@fontsource-variable\/noto-sans-devanagari/);
74	  });
75	  it("the font stack falls back to Noto for Kannada + Devanagari glyphs", () => {
76	    const css = readFileSync(join(SRC_DIR, "index.css"), "utf8");
77	    expect(css).toMatch(/Noto Sans Kannada Variable/);
78	    expect(css).toMatch(/Noto Sans Devanagari Variable/);
79	  });
80	});
81	
82	// The Studio SPA must render correctly on an AIR-GAPPED appliance (GPU/CPU/RAM, no internet) —
83	// every asset (fonts, styles, scripts) is bundled local, nothing pulled from a CDN/Google Fonts.
84	describe("offline / self-contained — no external runtime assets", () => {
85	  const CDN = /googleapis|gstatic|fonts\.google|cdnjs|unpkg|jsdelivr|\bcdn\.[a-z]/i;
86	  const FILES = [
87	    "../index.html", // web/index.html
88	    "main.tsx",
89	    "index.css",
90	  ];
91	  for (const rel of FILES) {
92	    it(`${rel}: no external font/CDN reference`, () => {
93	      expect(readFileSync(join(SRC_DIR, rel), "utf8")).not.toMatch(CDN);
94	    });
95	  }
96	  it("web/index.html loads no absolute-URL <link>/<script src> (everything is bundled/local)", () => {
97	    const html = readFileSync(join(SRC_DIR, "..", "index.html"), "utf8");
98	    expect(html).not.toMatch(/(?:href|src)\s*=\s*["']https?:\/\//i);
99	  });

1	// React state wrapper over the pure selection helpers in lib/selection.ts.
2	// Default = ALL selected (D8: curate-by-removal) — the user removes warm-ups.
3	import { useCallback, useMemo, useState } from "react";
4	import type { RallySegment } from "../api/types";
5	import * as sel from "../lib/selection";
6	
7	export function useRallySelection(rallies: RallySegment[], initial: "all" | "none" = "all") {
8	  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<number>>(() =>
9	    initial === "none" ? sel.clear() : sel.selectAll(rallies),
10	  );
11	
12	  const toggle = useCallback((id: number) => setSelected((s) => sel.toggle(s, id)), []);
13	  const selectAll = useCallback(() => setSelected(sel.selectAll(rallies)), [rallies]);
14	  const clearAll = useCallback(() => setSelected(sel.clear()), []);
15	  const invert = useCallback(() => setSelected((s) => sel.invert(s, rallies)), [rallies]);
16	  const isSelected = useCallback((id: number) => selected.has(id), [selected]);
17	  /** Replace the whole selection (the query bar writes the shared set here — D3). */
18	  const apply = useCallback((ids: Iterable<number>) => setSelected(new Set(ids)), []);
19	
20	  const totalSec = useMemo(() => sel.selectedDurationSec(rallies, selected), [rallies, selected]);
21	
22	  return { selected, count: selected.size, totalSec, isSelected, toggle, selectAll, clearAll, invert, apply };
23	}

1	// The fast-entry query bar (PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md P3). A deterministic, OFFLINE parser turns
2	// plain text ("top 15 longest", "over 10s") into a selection over the SAME shared set the
3	// checkboxes use. It is honest by construction: a live "parsed-pill" shows exactly how the
4	// text was understood, and concepts with no data behind them (shot-type / player / score) are
5	// greyed out as "not available yet" — never silently faked.
6	import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
7	import type { FormEvent } from "react";
8	import { useTranslation, Trans } from "react-i18next";
9	import { parseQuery, summarize } from "../lib/query";
10	import type { ParsedQuery } from "../lib/query";
11	
12	interface Props {
13	  /** Apply a parsed query to the rally set (App runs runQuery + writes the shared selection).
14	   * `source` distinguishes a typed-and-submitted query from an example-chip tap (for the trace). */
15	  onApply: (q: ParsedQuery, source: "typed" | "example") => void;
16	}
17	
18	// Each example is a real, parseable query — tapping one fills the box AND applies it. Led by rally
19	// LENGTH (the trusted axis); shot-count filtering exists but is experimental, so it's not featured
20	// here (a test pins that every example parses to a recognized, fully-available query).
21	export const EXAMPLES = ["over 10s", "top 15 longest", "shortest first", "first 10"];
22	
23	const CHIP = "rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium";
24	
25	export function QueryBar({ onApply }: Props) {
26	  const { t } = useTranslation();
27	  const [value, setValue] = useState("");
28	  const parsed = useMemo(() => parseQuery(value), [value]);
29	  const clauses = summarize(parsed);
30	  const typed = value.trim().length > 0;
31	
32	  const applyText = (text: string) => {
33	    setValue(text);
34	    onApply(parseQuery(text), "example");
35	  };
36	  const submit = (e: FormEvent) => {
37	    e.preventDefault();
38	    if (parsed.recognized) onApply(parsed, "typed");
39	  };
40	
41	  return (
42	    <div>
43	      <form onSubmit={submit} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
44	        <input
45	          type="text"
46	          value={value}
47	          onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)}
48	          placeholder={t("query.placeholder")}
49	          aria-label={t("query.ariaLabel")}
50	          className="min-w-[16rem] flex-1 rounded-xl border border-black/15 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm text-ink outline-none focus:border-green"
51	        />
52	        <button
53	          type="submit"
54	          disabled={!parsed.recognized}
55	          className="rounded-xl bg-ink px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-40"
56	        >
57	          {t("query.apply")}
58	        </button>
59	      </form>
60	
61	      {/* Live interpretation — transparent + hand-correctable (no hidden NL guessing). */}
62	      <div aria-live="polite" data-testid="query-pill" className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
63	        {clauses.map((c) => (
64	          <span key={c} className={CHIP + " bg-green/15 text-green"}>
65	            {c}
66	          </span>
67	        ))}
68	        {parsed.unavailable.map((u) => (
69	          <span
70	            key={u.token}
71	            title={t("query.notAvailableTitle", { token: u.token, kind: u.kind })}
72	            className={CHIP + " bg-black/5 text-ink/45"}
73	          >
74	            <span className="line-through">{u.token}</span> · {t("query.notAvailable")}
75	          </span>
76	        ))}
77	        {typed && !parsed.recognized && parsed.unavailable.length === 0 && (
78	          <span className="text-xs text-ink/50">{t("query.unreadable")}</span>
79	        )}
80	        {!typed && <span className="text-xs text-ink/50">{t("query.hint")}</span>}
81	      </div>
82	
83	      <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-sm">
84	        <span className="text-ink/40">{t("query.try")}</span>
85	        {EXAMPLES.map((ex) => (
86	          <button

I've now read every load-bearing surface and the workflow's map checks out exactly. Here's the complete B-P2 plan, then I'll build it.

## B-P2 — "point at a bucket video in the UI" (the full plan)

**Goal:** a user enters a `gs://…` URI (or engine-local path) in the SPA → it submits to `/api/process` → shows live progress while polling `/api/jobs/{id}` → on success loads that `video_id` into the existing rally-selection back-half. **100% `web/` SPA + i18n, zero engine code** (B-P1 already ships the endpoint).

**Engine contract (verified):** `POST /api/process {video_path}` → `202 {job_id, status:"queued", poll}` (fast-fails `400` bad/unsupported scheme, `404` missing local file); `GET /api/jobs/{id}` → `{status: queued|running|done|failed, progress, video_id, error, result:{status, video_id}}`. Two status layers: `job.status` (execution) vs `result.status` (pipeline verdict) — `done` + `result.status==="failed"`/no `video_id` is a success-shaped failure.

**Files:**
| File | Change |
|---|---|
| `web/src/api/types.ts` | + `ProcessResponse`, `JobStatus` |
| `web/src/api/client.ts` | + `processVideo()` (POST /process), `getJob()` (GET /jobs/{id}) — reuse the `request<T>`/`ApiError`/log pattern |
| `web/src/lib/errors.ts` *(new)* | `parseDetail` (moved out of App.tsx, reused) + `classifyError` → error codes (no i18n in logic) |
| `web/src/hooks/useIngestJob.ts` *(new)* | state machine `idle→submitting→polling→done\|error`; recursive `setTimeout` poll w/ backoff + ceiling; one `cid` threaded; cancel/unmount-safe; i18n-free (returns codes) |
| `web/src/components/IngestPanel.tsx` *(new)* | localized URI form + submit + `aria-live` progress + red error banner + Retry/Cancel; prop `onLoaded(videoId)` |
| `web/src/App.tsx` | `videoId` `useMemo`→`useState`; render `<IngestPanel>` when `!videoId`; `onLoaded` → `setVideoId` + `history.replaceState('?video='+id)`; import `parseDetail` from lib |
| `web/src/i18n/locales/{en,kn,hi}/common.json` | + `ingest.*` block (key-parity enforced; kn/hi AI-drafted, native-review-pending) |
| `web/src/i18n/hygiene.test.ts` | add `IngestPanel.tsx` to the no-bare-strings file list |
| `web/src/hooks/useIngestJob.test.ts` + `components/IngestPanel.test.tsx` *(new)* | mirror `client.test.ts` conventions |

**Decisions/risks handled:** recursive `setTimeout` (no request pile-up) + backoff 1s→5s + a ~10-min ceiling; success-shaped-failure → `emptyResult`; friendly `networkError` (this is the first screen a fresh user hits); progress is free-text phase words (indeterminate spinner, not a fake %); `gs://` URI demo preserved below the panel.

**DoD:** typecheck + all web tests green (incl. key-parity), live browser verification of submit→poll→load (mock-engine), then flip `DRY_RUN_CUJ.md` §6 DoD box 3.

### 206. assistant (2026-06-21T09:55:41.190Z)

// Error helpers shared by the ingest flow and the build flow. Kept i18n-FREE: callers map an
// IngestErrorCode to a localized string (so hooks stay testable without a translation context),
// while the engine's own `detail` text passes through verbatim (it's the most specific message).
import { ApiError } from "../api/client";

/** Pull FastAPI's {"detail": "..."} message out of an error body, else return the raw body. */
export function parseDetail(body: string): string {
  try {
    const j = JSON.parse(body) as { detail?: unknown };
    if (j && typeof j.detail === "string") return j.detail;
  } catch {
    /* not JSON — fall through to the raw body */
  }
  return body;
}

/** Stable, localizable failure categories surfaced by the ingest flow.
 *  `engine` carries the engine's own message in `detail`; the rest are localized by code. */
export type IngestErrorCode = "invalidUri" | "networkError" | "emptyResult" | "timeout" | "engine";

export interface IngestError {
  code: IngestErrorCode;
  /** Present for `engine` — the verbatim FastAPI detail / status; rendered as-is, not translated. */
  detail?: string;
}

/** Classify a thrown client error into a stable code. A network throw (ApiError with no `status`)
 *  is the common "engine unreachable" case → its own friendly code; any HTTP error surfaces the
 *  engine's `detail` (e.g. an unsupported-scheme 400, a not-found 404). */
export function classifyError(err: unknown): IngestError {
  if (err instanceof ApiError) {
    if (err.status === undefined) return { code: "networkError" };
    if (err.body) {
      const detail = parseDetail(err.body);
      if (detail) return { code: "engine", detail };
    }
    return { code: "engine", detail: `HTTP ${err.status}` };
  }
  return { code: "engine", detail: String(err) };
}

### 209. assistant (2026-06-21T09:56:33.275Z)

// Drives the front-half ingest: submit a video to POST /api/process, then poll GET /api/jobs/{id}
// until it finishes, and hand the resulting video_id to the existing rally-loading back-half.
//
// Kept i18n-FREE (returns stable IngestErrorCodes, not prose) so it unit-tests without a
// translation context — IngestPanel maps codes to localized strings. The poll loop is a RECURSIVE
// setTimeout (never setInterval): each tick waits for the prior getJob to resolve, so a slow engine
// can't pile up overlapping requests. It backs off, gives up after a ceiling, and is fully
// cancellable on unmount / user-cancel / a superseding submit.
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { getJob, processVideo } from "../api/client";
import { classifyError, type IngestError } from "../lib/errors";
import { log, newCorrelationId } from "../lib/log";
import type { JobStatus } from "../api/types";

const POLL_MIN_MS = 1000;
const POLL_MAX_MS = 5000;
const POLL_BACKOFF = 1.5;
const POLL_CEILING_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000; // a wedged 'running' job won't poll forever

export type IngestPhase =
  | { status: "idle" }
  | { status: "submitting" }
  | { status: "polling"; progress: string | null }
  | { status: "done"; videoId: string }
  | { status: "error"; error: IngestError };

interface Run {
  cancelled: boolean;
  timer?: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;
}

export function useIngestJob(onLoaded?: (videoId: string) => void) {
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<IngestPhase>({ status: "idle" });
  const runRef = useRef<Run | null>(null);
  // Keep the latest onLoaded without making submit depend on it (a parent re-render won't restart polls).
  const onLoadedRef = useRef(onLoaded);
  onLoadedRef.current = onLoaded;

  const stop = useCallback(() => {
    const run = runRef.current;
    if (run) {
      run.cancelled = true;
      if (run.timer) clearTimeout(run.timer);
    }
    runRef.current = null;
  }, []);

  // Cancel any in-flight poll loop when the component unmounts (App.tsx:80-82 alive-flag idiom).
  useEffect(() => stop, [stop]);

  const submit = useCallback(
    (rawUri: string) => {
      const uri = rawUri.trim();
      if (!uri) {
        setPhase({ status: "error", error: { code: "invalidUri" } });
        return;
      }

      stop(); // supersede any prior run
      const run: Run = { cancelled: false };
      runRef.current = run;

      const cid = newCorrelationId();
      const startedAt = Date.now();
      setPhase({ status: "submitting" });
      log("info", "ingest.submit_start", { cid, uri });

      const fail = (error: IngestError, event: string) => {
        if (run.cancelled) return;
        setPhase({ status: "error", error });
        const level = error.code === "timeout" || error.code === "emptyResult" ? "warn" : "error";
        log(level, event, { cid, uri, code: error.code, detail: error.detail });
      };

      const poll = (jobId: string, delay: number) => {
        run.timer = setTimeout(() => {
          void (async () => {
            if (run.cancelled) return;
            let job: JobStatus;
            try {
              job = await getJob(jobId, cid);
            } catch (err) {
              fail(classifyError(err), "ingest.poll_error");
              return;
            }
            if (run.cancelled) return;

            if (job.status === "done") {
              const videoId = job.video_id ?? job.result?.video_id ?? null;
              // Two status layers: a `done` job with a `failed` pipeline verdict (or no video_id) is
              // a success-shaped FAILURE — never load it, never hang.
              if (job.result?.status === "failed" || !videoId) {
                fail({ code: "emptyResult" }, "ingest.empty_result");
                return;
              }
              setPhase({ status: "done", videoId });
              log("info", "ingest.done", { cid, uri, video_id: videoId });
              onLoadedRef.current?.(videoId);
              return;
            }
            if (job.status === "failed") {
              fail({ code: "engine", detail: job.error ?? "job failed" }, "ingest.job_failed");
              return;
            }
            // queued | running → reflect coarse progress (free-text phase) and keep polling.
            setPhase({ status: "polling", progress: job.progress ?? null });
            if (Date.now() - startedAt > POLL_CEILING_MS) {
              fail({ code: "timeout" }, "ingest.poll_timeout");
              return;
            }
            poll(jobId, Math.min(delay * POLL_BACKOFF, POLL_MAX_MS));
          })();
        }, delay);
      };

      void (async () => {
        let jobId: string;
        try {
          const resp = await processVideo(uri, cid);
          jobId = resp.job_id;
        } catch (err) {
          fail(classifyError(err), "ingest.submit_error");
          return;
        }
        if (run.cancelled) return;
        log("info", "ingest.queued", { cid, uri, job_id: jobId });
        setPhase({ status: "polling", progress: null });
        poll(jobId, POLL_MIN_MS);
      })();
    },
    [stop],
  );

  const cancel = useCallback(() => {
    stop();
    setPhase({ status: "idle" });
    log("info", "ingest.cancel", {});
  }, [stop]);

  const reset = useCallback(() => {
    stop();
    setPhase({ status: "idle" });
  }, [stop]);

  const busy = phase.status === "submitting" || phase.status === "polling";
  return { phase, submit, cancel, reset, busy };
}

### 212. assistant (2026-06-21T09:57:27.173Z)

// The front-half ingest screen (appliance S1/S2, tracker B-P2). Point the tool at a video — a
// `gs://` bucket URI or a path on the engine host — and it submits to POST /api/process, shows
// live progress while polling GET /api/jobs/{id}, and hands the resulting video_id to the existing
// rally-selection back-half (App lifts ?video=<id>). Honest by construction: the engine reports
// coarse phase WORDS (not a %), and a success-shaped failure (done but no rallies) is shown, never
// silently treated as a load.
import { useState } from "react";
import type { FormEvent } from "react";
import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
import { useIngestJob } from "../hooks/useIngestJob";

interface Props {
  /** Called with the engine's video_id once a submitted job finishes successfully. */
  onLoaded: (videoId: string) => void;
}

export function IngestPanel({ onLoaded }: Props) {
  const { t } = useTranslation();
  const [uri, setUri] = useState("");
  const { phase, submit, cancel, reset, busy } = useIngestJob(onLoaded);

  const onSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    submit(uri);
  };

  // `engine` failures carry the engine's own message (a 400 scheme/path detail, a worker error);
  // every other code maps to a friendly localized line — the network case especially, since this
  // is the first screen a fresh user hits and "is the engine running?" must read plainly.
  const errorMsg =
    phase.status === "error"
      ? phase.error.code === "engine"
        ? (phase.error.detail ?? t("ingest.failed", { error: "" }))
        : t(`ingest.${phase.error.code}`)
      : null;

  return (
    <section className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-black/10 bg-white p-5" data-testid="ingest-panel">
      <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">{t("ingest.title")}</h2>
      <p className="mt-1 max-w-2xl text-sm text-ink/60">{t("ingest.intro")}</p>

      <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <input
          type="text"
          value={uri}
          onChange={(e) => setUri(e.target.value)}
          placeholder={t("ingest.uriPlaceholder")}
          aria-label={t("ingest.uriAriaLabel")}
          disabled={busy}
          className="min-w-[18rem] flex-1 rounded-xl border border-black/15 bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm text-ink outline-none focus:border-green disabled:opacity-50"
        />
        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={busy}
          className="rounded-xl bg-ink px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-40"
        >
          {phase.status === "submitting" ? t("ingest.submitting") : t("ingest.submit")}
        </button>
        {busy && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={cancel}
            className="rounded-xl border border-black/15 px-4 py-2 text-sm hover:border-green"
          >
            {t("ingest.cancel")}
          </button>
        )}
      </form>

      <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-ink/40">{t("ingest.hint")}</p>

      {/* Live status (aria-live so screen readers hear progress). Indeterminate: the engine reports
          phase words ("hashing", "segmenting"), not a percentage — so a spinner + the phase word. */}
      <div aria-live="polite" className="mt-3 text-sm" data-testid="ingest-status">
        {busy && (
          <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-ink/70">
            <Spinner />
            <span>
              {t("ingest.working")}
              {phase.status === "polling" && phase.progress
                ? ` · ${t("ingest.progress", { progress: phase.progress })}`
                : ""}
            </span>
          </p>
        )}
        {phase.status === "error" && (
          <div className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-3 py-2 text-red-800" data-testid="ingest-error">
            <p>{errorMsg}</p>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={reset}
              className="mt-2 rounded-lg border border-red-300 px-3 py-1 text-xs hover:bg-red-100"
            >
              {t("ingest.retry")}
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

function Spinner() {
  return (
    <span
      className="inline-block h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-ink/20 border-t-ink/70"
      aria-hidden="true"
    />
  );
}

=== scripts ===
{
  "dev": "vite",
  "build": "vite build",
  "preview": "vite preview",
  "typecheck": "tsc --noEmit",
  "test": "vitest run"
}
=== test setup ===
// Vitest setup (runs before each test file's imports). DOM-env only: jsdom under Node 26 does not
// expose a Web Storage `localStorage`, which the i18n language detector and the persistence test
// both need. Node-env test files (typeof window === "undefined") skip this entirely.
if (typeof window !== "undefined" && typeof globalThis.localStorage === "undefined") {
  let store: Record<string, string> = {};
  const mem: Storage = {
    get length() {
      return Object.keys(store).length;
    },
    clear() {
      store = {};
    },
    getItem(key: string) {
      return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(store, key) ? store[key] : null;
    },
    key(index: number) {
      return Object.keys(store)[index] ?? null;
    },
    removeItem(key: string) {
      delete store[key];
    },
    setItem(key: string, value: string) {
      store[key] = String(value);
    },
  };
  Object.defineProperty(window, "localStorage", { value: mem, configurable: true, writable: true });
  Object.defineProperty(globalThis, "localStorage", { value: mem, configurable: true, writable: true });
}

//
// The SPA had NO render tests before this (verified) — this file adds the jsdom safety net for the
// i18n scaffold (tracker B1): real copy renders from the catalog, the switcher persists the choice,
// missing-locale falls back to English, and ICU plurals format. It mounts the real <App />.
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from "vitest";
import { render, screen, cleanup, fireEvent } from "@testing-library/react";
import i18n, { LANG_STORAGE_KEY } from "./index";
import App from "../App";

beforeEach(async () => {
  // No engine in the test env: stub fetch so App's getConfig()/listRallies effects reject quietly
  // (App handles this gracefully — cfg stays null) instead of hitting the network.
  vi.stubGlobal("fetch", vi.fn(() => Promise.reject(new Error("no engine in test"))));
  window.localStorage.clear();
  await i18n.changeLanguage("en"); // also guarantees init completed before the first render
});

afterEach(() => {

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { parseDetail, classifyError } from "./errors";
import { ApiError } from "../api/client";

describe("parseDetail", () => {
  it("extracts FastAPI's {detail}", () => {
    expect(parseDetail(JSON.stringify({ detail: "unsupported storage URI scheme" }))).toBe(
      "unsupported storage URI scheme",
    );
  });
  it("returns the raw body when it isn't JSON or has no string detail", () => {
    expect(parseDetail("plain text")).toBe("plain text");
    expect(parseDetail(JSON.stringify({ other: 1 }))).toBe(JSON.stringify({ other: 1 }));
    expect(parseDetail(JSON.stringify({ detail: { nested: true } }))).toBe(JSON.stringify({ detail: { nested: true } }));
  });
});

describe("classifyError", () => {
  it("a network throw (ApiError with no status) → networkError", () => {
    expect(classifyError(new ApiError("net", { cid: "c" }))).toEqual({ code: "networkError" });
  });
  it("an HTTP error with a body → engine + the FastAPI detail", () => {
    const err = new ApiError("x", { status: 400, body: JSON.stringify({ detail: "unsupported storage URI scheme" }) });
    expect(classifyError(err)).toEqual({ code: "engine", detail: "unsupported storage URI scheme" });
  });
  it("an HTTP error with no body → engine + the HTTP status", () => {
    expect(classifyError(new ApiError("x", { status: 500 }))).toEqual({ code: "engine", detail: "HTTP 500" });
  });
  it("a non-ApiError → engine + the stringified error", () => {
    expect(classifyError(new Error("boom"))).toEqual({ code: "engine", detail: "Error: boom" });
  });
});

### 247. assistant (2026-06-21T10:04:51.754Z)

// @vitest-environment jsdom
//
// The ingest state machine: submit → poll (queued/running) → done | error. Drives the recursive
// setTimeout poll loop with fake timers so each tick is deterministic, and pins the load-bearing
// behaviours: the success-shaped-failure (done + no rallies → emptyResult, never a load), the
// two error surfaces (engine detail vs friendly network), and cancellation stopping the loop.
import { describe, it, expect, vi, afterEach } from "vitest";
import { renderHook, act } from "@testing-library/react";
import { useIngestJob } from "./useIngestJob";
import type { JobStatus } from "../api/types";

type FetchFn = typeof fetch;

function httpOk(status: number, json: unknown) {
  return { ok: status >= 200 && status < 300, status, json: async () => json, text: async () => JSON.stringify(json) };
}

/** A fetch that answers POST /api/process with `process`, then walks `jobs` on each GET /api/jobs/*. */
function fetchSeq(process: unknown, jobs: JobStatus[]): FetchFn {
  let i = 0;
  return vi.fn(async (url: string | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
    const u = String(url);
    const method = init?.method ?? "GET";
    if (u === "/api/process" && method === "POST") return process;
    if (u.startsWith("/api/jobs/")) {
      const body = jobs[Math.min(i, jobs.length - 1)];
      i += 1;
      return httpOk(200, body);
    }
    throw new Error(`unexpected fetch ${method} ${u}`);
  }) as unknown as FetchFn;
}

/** Flush the submit microtasks, then walk the backoff ladder (1000 → 1500 → 2250 → …) for n ticks. */
async function drain(ticks: number) {
  await act(async () => {
    await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0); // flush processVideo → schedule the first poll
  });
  let delay = 1000;
  for (let k = 0; k < ticks; k += 1) {
    await act(async () => {
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(delay);
    });
    delay = Math.min(delay * 1.5, 5000);
  }
}

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

describe("useIngestJob", () => {
  it("rejects an empty URI before any network call", () => {
    const f = vi.fn();
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", f);
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useIngestJob());
    act(() => result.current.submit("   "));
    expect(result.current.phase).toEqual({ status: "error", error: { code: "invalidUri" } });
    expect(f).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("submits, polls queued → running → done, and hands off the video_id", async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    vi.stubGlobal(
      "fetch",
      fetchSeq(httpOk(202, { job_id: "j1", status: "queued", poll: "/api/jobs/j1" }), [
        { job_id: "j1", status: "queued" },
        { job_id: "j1", status: "running", progress: "segmenting (gemini)" },
        { job_id: "j1", status: "done", video_id: "vid9", result: { status: "success", video_id: "vid9" } },
      ]),
    );
    const onLoaded = vi.fn();
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useIngestJob(onLoaded));
    await act(async () => {
      result.current.submit("gs://b/x.mp4");
    });
    await drain(3);
    expect(onLoaded).toHaveBeenCalledWith("vid9");
    expect(result.current.phase).toEqual({ status: "done", videoId: "vid9" });
  });

  it("treats a done-but-failed pipeline verdict as emptyResult — never a load", async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    vi.stubGlobal(
      "fetch",
      fetchSeq(httpOk(202, { job_id: "j2", status: "queued", poll: "/api/jobs/j2" }), [
        { job_id: "j2", status: "done", video_id: "vidX", result: { status: "failed" } },
      ]),
    );
    const onLoaded = vi.fn();
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useIngestJob(onLoaded));
    await act(async () => {
      result.current.submit("gs://b/x.mp4");
    });
    await drain(1);
    expect(onLoaded).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(result.current.phase).toEqual({ status: "error", error: { code: "emptyResult" } });
  });

  it("surfaces an execution failure with the engine's error text", async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    vi.stubGlobal(
      "fetch",
      fetchSeq(httpOk(202, { job_id: "j3", status: "queued", poll: "/api/jobs/j3" }), [
        { job_id: "j3", status: "failed", error: "RuntimeError: boom" },
      ]),
    );
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useIngestJob());
    await act(async () => {
      result.current.submit("gs://b/x.mp4");
    });
    await drain(1);
    expect(result.current.phase).toEqual({ status: "error", error: { code: "engine", detail: "RuntimeError: boom" } });
  });

  it("maps a 400 from /api/process to the engine's detail", async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", fetchSeq(httpOk(400, { detail: "unsupported storage URI scheme" }), []));
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useIngestJob());
    await act(async () => {
      result.current.submit("ftp://nope");
    });
    await act(async () => {
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
    });
    expect(result.current.phase).toEqual({
      status: "error",
      error: { code: "engine", detail: "unsupported storage URI scheme" },
    });
  });

  it("maps an unreachable engine to a friendly networkError", async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", vi.fn(async () => {
      throw new Error("offline");
    }) as unknown as FetchFn);
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useIngestJob());
    await act(async () => {
      result.current.submit("gs://b/x.mp4");
    });
    await act(async () => {
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
    });
    expect(result.current.phase).toEqual({ status: "error", error: { code: "networkError" } });
  });

  it("cancel stops the poll loop and returns to idle", async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    vi.stubGlobal(
      "fetch",
      fetchSeq(httpOk(202, { job_id: "j4", status: "queued", poll: "/api/jobs/j4" }), [
        { job_id: "j4", status: "running", progress: "hashing" },
      ]),
    );
    const onLoaded = vi.fn();
    const { result } = renderHook(() => useIngestJob(onLoaded));
    await act(async () => {
      result.current.submit("gs://b/x.mp4");
    });
    await drain(1);
    expect(result.current.phase.status).toBe("polling");
    act(() => result.current.cancel());
    expect(result.current.phase).toEqual({ status: "idle" });
    // Further time passes; nothing resumes, onLoaded never fires.
    await act(async () => {
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(10000);
    });
    expect(result.current.phase).toEqual({ status: "idle" });
    expect(onLoaded).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

### 252. assistant (2026-06-21T10:05:40.399Z)

// @vitest-environment jsdom
//
// The ingest screen end to end: type a URI → submit → (mocked engine) → onLoaded gets the video_id;
// and the two failure surfaces a non-technical user actually hits — the engine's own 400 detail,
// and a friendly "is the engine running?" when it's unreachable. Mounts the real i18n catalog.
import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
import { render, screen, fireEvent, cleanup, act } from "@testing-library/react";
import i18n from "../i18n/index";
import { IngestPanel } from "./IngestPanel";
import type { JobStatus } from "../api/types";

type FetchFn = typeof fetch;

function httpOk(status: number, json: unknown) {
  return { ok: status >= 200 && status < 300, status, json: async () => json, text: async () => JSON.stringify(json) };
}

function fetchSeq(process: unknown, jobs: JobStatus[]): FetchFn {
  let i = 0;
  return vi.fn(async (url: string | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
    const u = String(url);
    if (u === "/api/process" && (init?.method ?? "GET") === "POST") return process;
    if (u.startsWith("/api/jobs/")) {
      const body = jobs[Math.min(i, jobs.length - 1)];
      i += 1;
      return httpOk(200, body);
    }
    throw new Error(`unexpected fetch ${u}`);
  }) as unknown as FetchFn;
}

beforeEach(async () => {
  window.localStorage.clear();
  await i18n.changeLanguage("en"); // guarantees init completed + a known locale for the assertions
});

afterEach(() => {
  cleanup();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
  vi.unstubAllGlobals();
  vi.useRealTimers();
});

const typeUri = (value: string) =>
  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText(/video location/i), { target: { value } });
const clickProcess = () => fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: /process video/i }));

describe("IngestPanel", () => {
  it("submits a URI, polls, and hands the video_id to onLoaded", async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    vi.stubGlobal(
      "fetch",
      fetchSeq(httpOk(202, { job_id: "j", status: "queued", poll: "/api/jobs/j" }), [
        { job_id: "j", status: "running", progress: "segmenting" },
        { job_id: "j", status: "done", video_id: "vidZ", result: { status: "success", video_id: "vidZ" } },
      ]),
    );
    const onLoaded = vi.fn();
    render(<IngestPanel onLoaded={onLoaded} />);
    typeUri("gs://bucket/match.mp4");
    clickProcess();

    await act(async () => {
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0); // flush submit → start polling
    });
    await act(async () => {
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1000); // running tick
    });
    await act(async () => {
      await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1500); // done tick
    });
    expect(onLoaded).toHaveBeenCalledWith("vidZ");
  });

  it("shows the engine's own 400 detail (e.g. an unsupported scheme)", async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", fetchSeq(httpOk(400, { detail: "unsupported storage URI scheme: ftp://" }), []));
    render(<IngestPanel onLoaded={vi.fn()} />);
    typeUri("ftp://nope");
    clickProcess();
    expect(await screen.findByText(/unsupported storage URI scheme/i)).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("shows a friendly, localized message when the engine is unreachable", async () => {
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", vi.fn(async () => {
      throw new Error("offline");
    }) as unknown as FetchFn);
    render(<IngestPanel onLoaded={vi.fn()} />);
    typeUri("gs://bucket/match.mp4");
    clickProcess();
    expect(await screen.findByText(/can't reach the engine/i)).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("rejects an empty URI with a hint and never calls the engine", () => {
    const f = vi.fn();
    vi.stubGlobal("fetch", f);
    render(<IngestPanel onLoaded={vi.fn()} />);
    clickProcess();
    expect(screen.getByTestId("ingest-error")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(f).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

Good live signal — and it surfaced a UX nuance worth fixing. With no engine on `:8000`, the Vite proxy returns a bare **HTTP 500** (empty body), and the panel currently shows the cryptic "HTTP 500" to the user. For this non-technical audience, a bare 5xx with no detail almost always means "engine down/unreachable" — it should get the friendly "is the engine running?" message, while any error *with* a real detail still shows that detail. ### 309. assistant (2026-06-21T10:14:33.372Z)

Done. **B-P2 is built, verified, and up as [khelsutra#56](https://github.com/avidullu/khelsutra/pull/56) with green CI.** The full plan is at the top of this turn; here's what landed against it.

## What shipped

A user can now point at a bucket video **entirely in the UI** — no CLI pre-step:

`IngestPanel` (enter `gs://…`) → `POST /api/process` → poll `GET /api/jobs/{id}` (recursive `setTimeout`, backoff + 10-min ceiling, one correlation id) → on `done`, hand `result.video_id` to `App` → `?video=` is set → the existing rally-selection back-half loads. **100% `web/` + i18n, zero engine code** (it rides B-P1's already-shipped endpoint).

New: [api/client.ts](web/src/api/client.ts) (`processVideo`/`getJob`), [lib/errors.ts](web/src/lib/errors.ts) (`parseDetail` de-duped + `classifyError`), [hooks/useIngestJob.ts](web/src/hooks/useIngestJob.ts) (the state machine), [components/IngestPanel.tsx](web/src/components/IngestPanel.tsx), plus `App.tsx` wiring and `ingest.*` in en/kn/hi.

## Verification
- **123 web tests** (+19: error classifier, hook state-machine with a fake-timer poll loop, panel integration), `tsc` clean, `vite build` green, key-parity green.
- **Browser-verified** live: panel renders in **en + hi**, and a submit with no engine shows the friendly localized message (हिन्दी: *"इंजन तक नहीं पहुँच पा रहे। क्या यह चल रहा है?"* / EN: *"Can't reach the engine. Is it running?"*), with structured correlation-id logs.
- **One UX nuance fixed from the live run:** a bare 5xx / dev-proxy-down was showing a cryptic "HTTP 500" — now a 5xx with no body maps to the friendly `networkError`, while a real engine detail (e.g. a 400 unsupported-scheme) still passes through verbatim.

## Honest status
- I left **#56 for you to merge** — it's a user-facing product change, and the working agreement keeps product gated to you (there's precedent for me merging SPA PRs on your "merge as you go," so just say the word). CI is green.
- `DRY_RUN_CUJ.md` §6 box 3 stays **◐** — the screen is built + tested, but it only flips on a **live e2e-from-UI run against a real engine** (local, or the droplet — option 3).
- kn/hi `ingest.*` strings are **AI-drafts pending native review** (consistent with the existing catalogs).

## Heads-up
A **concurrent session is actively working the engine-serving M5+ track** and writing to the shared memory/checkout (it's aware of my #284). No conflict — I appended to my own memory block and stayed off its branches.

### 342. user (2026-06-21T13:07:10.048Z)

1	// KhelSutra Studio i18n screencast: a Kannada-DEFAULT browser auto-renders Kannada,
2	// then the switcher walks kn -> hi -> en. Proves the owner's success criterion.
3	import { chromium } from "playwright";
4	
5	const URL = process.env.URL || "http://localhost:5180";
6	const KN_TITLE = "ನಿಮ್ಮ ರೀಲ್‌ಗಾಗಿ ರ್ಯಾಲಿಗಳನ್ನು ಆರಿಸಿ";
7	const HI_TITLE = "अपनी रील के लिए रैलियाँ चुनें";
8	const EN_TITLE = "Pick the rallies for your reel";
9	const HOLD = 2600;
10	
11	const browser = await chromium.launch();
12	// A Kannada-configured browser: locale sets navigator.language=kn-IN. Fresh context => no stored
13	// preference => the SPA negotiates from the BROWSER language (the whole point).
14	const ctx = await browser.newContext({
15	  locale: "kn-IN",
16	  viewport: { width: 1280, height: 900 },
17	  recordVideo: { dir: "video", size: { width: 1280, height: 900 } },
18	});
19	const page = await ctx.newPage();
20	await page.goto(URL, { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded" });
21	
22	// 1) Kannada renders automatically (browser language → kn).
23	await page.getByText(KN_TITLE, { exact: true }).waitFor({ timeout: 15000 });
24	console.log("kn rendered from browser language:", await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.lang));
25	await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
26	await page.screenshot({ path: "shot-1-kn.png" });
27	
28	// 2) Switch to Hindi.
29	await page.locator('button[lang="hi"]').click();
30	await page.getByText(HI_TITLE, { exact: true }).waitFor({ timeout: 8000 });
31	console.log("hi rendered:", await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.lang));
32	await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
33	await page.screenshot({ path: "shot-2-hi.png" });
34	
35	// 3) Switch to English.
36	await page.locator('button[lang="en"]').click();
37	await page.getByText(EN_TITLE, { exact: true }).waitFor({ timeout: 8000 });
38	console.log("en rendered:", await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.lang));
39	await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
40	await page.screenshot({ path: "shot-3-en.png" });
41	
42	await ctx.close(); // flushes the video
43	await browser.close();
44	console.log("DONE");
45

### 345. user (2026-06-21T13:07:48.154Z)

1	// The compiled-reel result (PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md P5): once Build → POST /api/compile succeeds,
2	// preview the reel inline (<video> over GET /api/highlights/...) and offer a download. The engine
3	// returns a server-local filepath, NOT a URL (main.py:394), so the SPA builds the URL itself. On a
4	// failure (e.g. the min_shot_count 400) we show the engine's reason — loud, never swallowed.
5	
6	import { useTranslation } from "react-i18next";
7	
8	export type BuildState =
9	  | { status: "idle" }
10	  | { status: "building" }
11	  | { status: "done"; url: string; filename: string }
12	  | { status: "error"; error: string };
13	
14	interface Props {
15	  build: BuildState;
16	  onDismiss: () => void;
17	}
18	
19	export function CompileResult({ build, onDismiss }: Props) {
20	  const { t } = useTranslation();
21	  if (build.status === "idle" || build.status === "building") return null;
22	
23	  return (
24	    <section
25	      data-testid="compile-result"
26	      className={
27	        "mt-6 rounded-xl border p-4 " +
28	        (build.status === "error" ? "border-red-200 bg-red-50" : "border-green/30 bg-green/5")
29	      }
30	    >
31	      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
32	        <h2 className="font-semibold text-ink">
33	          {build.status === "done" ? t("result.ready") : t("result.failed")}
34	        </h2>
35	        <button
36	          type="button"
37	          onClick={onDismiss}
38	          aria-label={t("result.dismiss")}
39	          className="rounded-full px-2 text-ink/50 hover:text-ink"
40	        >
41	          ✕
42	        </button>
43	      </div>
44	
45	      {build.status === "done" ? (
46	        <div className="mt-3">
47	          {/* eslint-disable-next-line jsx-a11y/media-has-caption — a stitched rally reel has no captions */}
48	          <video
49	            data-testid="reel-video"
50	            src={build.url}
51	            controls
52	            playsInline
53	            className="w-full max-w-xl rounded-lg bg-black"
54	          />
55	          <a
56	            href={build.url}
57	            download={build.filename}

SPA dev server is up on :5180 and proxies `/api` to the live engine. The download is an `<a download>` link, reel preview is `[data-testid="reel-video"]`. Let me write the CUJ recording script:

### 348. assistant (2026-06-21T13:08:38.034Z)

// KhelSutra Studio — FULL bucket→reel CUJ, driven entirely from the UI (B-P2).
// Point at a gs:// bucket video → engine processes (real Gemini) → review rallies → build → download.
// Records the whole journey. Run against the SPA dev server (proxies /api → the live engine on :8000).
import { chromium } from "playwright";

const URL = process.env.URL || "http://localhost:5180";
const GS_URI = "gs://khelsutra-rally-corpus/videos/mahadevpura_smoke_180s.mp4";
const PROCESS_TIMEOUT = 300000; // up to 5 min for the real Gemini run + gs:// fetch
const HOLD = 1800;

const browser = await chromium.launch();
// Fresh context, English browser → the SPA negotiates `en` (no persisted pref).
const ctx = await browser.newContext({
  locale: "en-US",
  viewport: { width: 1280, height: 900 },
  recordVideo: { dir: "video-cuj", size: { width: 1280, height: 900 } },
});
const page = await ctx.newPage();
page.on("console", (m) => { if (m.type() === "error") console.log("  [page error]", m.text().slice(0, 160)); });

await page.goto(URL, { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded" });

// 1) Land on the ingest screen (no ?video= → the "Process a video" panel).
await page.getByText("Process a video", { exact: true }).waitFor({ timeout: 15000 });
console.log("ingest panel rendered; lang =", await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.lang));
await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
await page.screenshot({ path: "cuj-1-ingest.png" });

// 2) Paste the bucket URI (typed for the camera) and submit.
const input = page.getByLabel(/video location/i);
await input.click();
await input.pressSequentially(GS_URI, { delay: 28 });
await page.waitForTimeout(700);
await page.screenshot({ path: "cuj-2-uri.png" });
await page.getByRole("button", { name: /process video/i }).click();

// 3) Live progress while the engine fetches the bucket video + runs Gemini.
await page.getByText(/Processing your video/i).waitFor({ timeout: 15000 }).catch(() => {});
await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
await page.screenshot({ path: "cuj-3-processing.png" });

// 4) Rallies load into the existing back-half (the ingest panel hands off video_id → ?video=).
await page.getByText(/rallies from this session/i).waitFor({ timeout: PROCESS_TIMEOUT });
const loaded = await page.getByText(/rallies from this session/i).textContent();
console.log("rallies loaded:", loaded?.trim());
await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
await page.screenshot({ path: "cuj-4-rallies.png" });

// 5) Build the reel from the (default all-selected) rallies.
const buildBtn = page.getByRole("button", { name: /build reel/i });
await buildBtn.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
await buildBtn.click();

// 6) Reel ready → preview + download.
await page.getByText("Your reel is ready", { exact: true }).waitFor({ timeout: 120000 });
await page.getByTestId("compile-result").scrollIntoViewIfNeeded();
await page.waitForTimeout(HOLD);
await page.screenshot({ path: "cuj-5-reel-ready.png" });

// 7) Download to the local machine (the real end of the CUJ).
const [download] = await Promise.all([
  page.waitForEvent("download"),
  page.getByRole("link", { name: /download/i }).click(),
]);
await download.saveAs("cuj-downloaded-reel.mp4");
console.log("downloaded reel:", download.suggestedFilename());
await page.waitForTimeout(1200);

await ctx.close(); // flushes the video
await browser.close();
console.log("DONE");

### 356. assistant (2026-06-21T13:14:55.411Z)

The process path resolves URIs via `acquire_local_input`; the compile path doesn't. **This is the real gap that completes the bucket→reel CUJ** — exactly what proof-driven validation is for.

## 🔬 Proof-driven development paid off immediately
Recording the live CUJ **surfaced a real bug** that unit tests and the prior Path-A run had missed: `/api/compile` **500'd** on a bucket-ingested video — `videos.filepath` keeps the `gs://` source URI (M2 provenance), and the stitcher was handed that directly, so ffmpeg couldn't open it. I fixed it (compile now resolves the source via `acquire_local_input` first — identity for local, fetch-to-scratch for a bucket URI), added a regression test, **re-ran the CUJ to confirm the reel builds + downloads**, and only then recorded the final screencast. That's the loop working as intended: *the attempt to prove it is what found the gap.*

+19 web tests (B-P2) and +1 engine regression test (`test_compile_resolves_bucket_source_to_local_before_stitching`). Engine suite **1182 passed / 7 skipped**; web **123 tests**; ruff + mypy clean across both.

One Job execution runs the detect+stitch unit. Rationale: it fits the async upload→poll→reel flow + scale-to-zero, and reuses the existing async job control plane verbatim (JobWaiting / claim_due_job / ExecutionPolicy WAIT_AND_RESUME). Implemented as backend/compute/CloudRunCompute (backend/compute/cloud_run.py): get_compute_backend(config) returns a backend ONLY when compute_target=cloud_run (else None → in-process path, default-OFF).\n\nGPU vs CPU: GPU-native, NVIDIA L4 (cuda), detector_impl=native (the Linux-native WASB runner). DETECT runs on GPU. WINDOW is a CPU-pure function (trajectory_to_action_windows). STITCH is real CPU/ffmpeg work and is CO-LOCATED in the SAME Cloud Run GPU job (D4/D16) so gpu_active_seconds (detect) + wall_seconds (detect+stitch) + bytes_out (reel egress) all land on ONE metered invocation → the per-job cost ledger. Stitch-on-laptop would hide that cost. Codec (D16): libx264 ENCODE (protects the D5 byte-determinism / parity guarantee) + NVDEC for DECODE only (speed lever, never touches output bytes); NVENC encode is DEFERRED behind a config knob. cpu-mock profile (detector_impl=stub) runs the whole thing GPU-free for $0 CI.\n\nSCALE-TO-ZERO: D7 — accept the ~1–3 min cold-start (amortized over a multi-minute async job); NO warm pool (a ~$500/mo idle pool would break per-video billing). Revisit a warm lane only on a UX complaint.\n\nDISPATCH + COMPLETION DETECTION: The control plane (DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE=cloud-serving → compute_target=cloud_run) dispatches a Cloud Run Job execution (injectable client), then BUCKET-POLLS a done-marker for completion — NOT the Cloud Run API status. The marker is &lt;out_uri&gt;/_dispatch/&lt;token&gt;.done, polled via execution_policy.poll_until + the storage layer, so it reuses JobWaiting/claim_due_job and survives a control-plane restart (D16). The dispatched container runs INLINE — CloudRunCompute forces compute_target=local_gpu + indexing.detector_impl=native in the per-execution args so the Job never re-dispatches itself; the worker writes the --done-uri marker on success. Known gap (follow-up): a FAILED job currently writes NO marker, so the dispatcher's bounded poll times out (ExecutionPolicy.max_wait_sec → clean rc 4); a failure-marker carrying the real non-zero rc (to distinguish failed from hung/cold-start-slow) is deferred. Reels returned via signed URL. Input cap: hard-reject &lt;2GB for v1 (a config cap, raise to 4GB later in one line, matching the M2 chunked-upload threshold).\n\nCONTAINERIZATION + THE WASB conda-env CONCERN: deploy/cloudrun/Dockerfile builds the L4 image FROM nvidia/cuda:12.4.1-runtime-ubuntu22.04, adds ffmpeg (stitch/decode) + DejaVu fonts (watermark filter). It deliberately carries TWO separate python stacks to avoid a framework conflict (same split as the native GPU box, deploy/digitalocean/gpu_setup.sh): (1) the MAIN app env (this repo + ffmpeg) drives the pipeline + stitching; (2) a conda 'wasb' env (python 3.8 + torch 1.11.0+cu113 / torchvision 0.12.0+cu113, from nttcom/WASB-SBDT) runs the WASB shuttle detector as a SUBPROCESS — the native runner shells out to $WASB_PYTHON so the app's torch never co-installs with WASB's torch-1.11. ENV wires WASB_PYTHON=/opt/conda/envs/wasb/bin/python, WASB_REPO_DIR, WASB_WEIGHTS, RALLY_DETECTOR_IMPL=native; ENTRYPOINT is python3 -m backend.worker. This dual-runtime packaging is exactly the \"known hard part\" ADR-012 flagged. WASB-C3 concern: the pretrained badminton weights are academic-data-trained, provenance NOT cleared for commercial sharing — they are NOT baked into the image; they are STAGED AT RUNTIME (bucket fetch or mount) with $WASB_WEIGHTS pointed at them. WASB-C3 must be resolved before any public, non-owner serving. CI does NOT build this image (a CUDA+conda image is too heavy); the dispatch shim is proven with a fake client (tests/test_compute_backend.py). The real build/push/L4 run is the M7 owner step under the D17 budget.","trackerFile":"C:/Users/avidu/Projects/badminton-highlight-indexer/docs/COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING/README.md — §7 \"Deliverables &amp; progress tracker\" is the explicit source of truth (legend: ☐ Todo · ◐ In progress · ☑ Done · ⛔ Gated).
